# Nathan Direct: 2023–2024 Per-Message Source and Topic Locator

**2026-10-24 QA edition.** **802 canonical role=user records across 43 conversation IDs**, identified from the existing Nathan Direct year shards. No user text has been republished here. Tags are machine-generated and distinguish direct-message evidence from adjacency inherited from other speakers. This is **not** an exhaustive census of every 2023–2024 original-archive TXT or non-JSON document.

- [2023 original user-message shard](https://github.com/Satobloc/HsH/blob/main/indexes/nathan-direct/nathan-direct-2023.jsonl) — 305 records / 16 conversation IDs.
- [2024 original user-message shard](https://github.com/Satobloc/HsH/blob/main/indexes/nathan-direct/nathan-direct-2024.jsonl) — 497 records / 27 conversation IDs.
- [Global message lookup](https://github.com/Satobloc/HsH/blob/main/indexes/nathan-direct/nathan-direct-lookup.jsonl) — 20,575 records across all years.
- [Raw conversation / filename subject tags](https://github.com/Satobloc/HsH/blob/main/indexes/autotag/CONVERSATION_TAG_INDEX.md) — separate, lower-precision discovery layer.
- **Known gap:** `SAT_THEORY_ARCHIVE_2023-25/000 Earliest SAT_RMS/Code-Like Text Style Emulation - bellomy - to Oct-2023.txt` contains 82 clearly delimited user turns, but that early conversation does not appear in the 2023 JSON-derived shard. The 802-message total is complete for these **existing year shards only**.

## Rules and outstanding coverage checks

1. `author_role:user` is based on raw ChatGPT conversation mapping; a quoted or pasted passage inside a user message can have a separate original author. Never infer origination of embedded quotes from transport role alone.
2. This metadata index does not make claims about SAT correctness or a first occurrence. Find source context and actual earlier citations before elevating tags.
3. Source title/path or `year` classification follows message timestamps; long conversations may cross years (including later 2026 revisits).
4. Raw conversation branches matter. Use raw parent/child pointers from the user shard and full original conversation instead of chronological adjacency alone.
5. The raw-JSON archive is not the original archive's complete TXT, notebook, image, SRT, or PDF corpus. Build additional cross-format intake and preserve provenance and original wording.
6. This file is a pointer index only. Actual verified compendium quotes, manually reviewed ledgers, and theorist feed handoffs are separately indexed at [the White-Glove Locator](https://github.com/Satobloc/HsH/blob/main/WORKSPACES/COMMON/NATHAN_DIRECT_WHITE_GLOVE_LOCATOR_INDEX_2026-10-24.md).
